| `policies` | json | Access policies |
| `groups` | json | Access groups in the account |
| `identity_providers` | json | Access identity providers in the account |
| `service_tokens` | json | Access service tokens in the account |
| `domain` | string | Hostname and path secured by Access |
| `aud` | string | Access application audience tag |
| `session_duration` | string | How long an Access session stays valid |
| `allowed_idps` | json | Identity provider IDs allowed on the application |
| `app_launcher_visible` | boolean | Whether the application appears in the App Launcher |
| `auto_redirect_to_identity` | boolean | Whether users skip the identity provider picker |
| `custom_deny_message` | string | Message shown when access is denied |
| `custom_deny_url` | string | URL denied users are redirected to |
| `logo_url` | string | Application logo URL |
| `self_hosted_domains` | json | Additional hostnames and paths secured by the application |
| `destinations` | json | Destinations secured by the application |
| `decision` | string | Access policy decision |
| `precedence` | number | Access policy evaluation order |
| `include` | json | Access rules evaluated with OR logic |
| `exclude` | json | Access rules evaluated with NOT logic |
| `require` | json | Access rules evaluated with AND logic |
| `approval_required` | boolean | Whether an approver must grant each access request |
| `isolation_required` | boolean | Whether sessions must run in a remote isolated browser |
| `purpose_justification_required` | boolean | Whether users must state a reason for access |
| `purpose_justification_prompt` | string | Prompt shown when a justification is required |
| `created_at` | string | Creation timestamp |
| `updated_at` | string | Last update timestamp |
| `client_id` | string | Service token client ID |
| `client_secret` | string | Service token client secret, returned only once at creation |
| `expires_at` | string | Service token expiry timestamp |
| `last_seen_at` | string | When the service token was last used |
| `duration` | string | Service token lifetime |
| `enabled` | boolean | Whether the resource is enabled |
| `buckets` | json | R2 buckets in the account |
| `creation_date` | string | R2 bucket creation timestamp |
| `location` | string | R2 bucket location |
| `storage_class` | string | R2 bucket default storage class |
| `jurisdiction` | string | R2 data-residency jurisdiction |
| `cursor` | string | Pagination cursor for the next page |
| `scripts` | json | Workers scripts in the account |
| `routes` | json | Workers routes on the zone |
| `bindings` | json | Resource bindings available to a Workers script |
| `compatibility_date` | string | Workers runtime compatibility date |
| `compatibility_flags` | json | Workers runtime compatibility flags |
| `limits` | json | Workers execution limits |
| `logpush` | boolean | Whether Workers Logpush is enabled |
| `migrations` | json | Durable Object migrations |
| `observability` | json | Workers observability configuration |
| `placement` | json | Workers smart placement configuration |
| `tail_consumers` | json | Workers consuming tail events |
| `usage_model` | string | Workers billing usage model |
| `tunnels` | json | Cloudflare Tunnels in the account |
| `tunnel_id` | string | Tunnel the configuration belongs to |
| `account_id` | string | Account the tunnel belongs to |
| `account_tag` | string | Account tag the tunnel belongs to |
| `config` | json | Tunnel ingress and origin request configuration |
| `config_src` | string | Where the tunnel configuration is managed |
| `source` | string | Where the tunnel configuration is managed |
| `tun_type` | string | Tunnel type |
| `remote_config` | boolean | Whether the tunnel is remotely managed |
| `deleted_at` | string | Tunnel deletion timestamp |
| `conns_active_at` | string | When the tunnel last had active connections |
| `conns_inactive_at` | string | When the tunnel last lost all connections |
| `connections` | json | Active connector connections for the tunnel |
| `oauth_configuration` | json | **Beta:** Optional configuration for managing an OAuth authorization flow controlled by Access. |
| `user_populations` | json | The single user population associated with this application. |
| `allow_authenticate_via_warp` | boolean | When set to true, users can authenticate to this application using their WARP session. When set to false this application will always require direct IdP authentication. This setting always overrides the organization setting for WARP authentication. |
| `allow_iframe` | boolean | Enables loading application content in an iFrame. |
| `cors_headers` | json | cors headers |
| `custom_non_identity_deny_url` | string | The custom URL a user is redirected to when they are denied access to the application when failing non-identity rules. |
| `custom_pages` | json | The custom pages that will be displayed when applicable for this application |
| `eager_redirect_cookie_setting` | boolean | Preemptively sets the Access session cookie on every hostname in a multi-hostname self-hosted application during the initial redirect chain, rather than setting it lazily on first visit. Defaults to true. Set to false to disable the eager redirect cookie behavior. |
| `enable_binding_cookie` | boolean | Enables the binding cookie, which increases security against compromised authorization tokens and CSRF attacks. |
| `http_only_cookie_attribute` | boolean | Enables the HttpOnly cookie attribute, which increases security against XSS attacks. |
| `mfa_config` | json | Configures multi-factor authentication \(MFA\) settings. |
| `options_preflight_bypass` | boolean | Allows options preflight requests to bypass Access authentication and go directly to the origin. Cannot turn on if cors_headers is set. |
| `path_cookie_attribute` | boolean | Enables cookie paths to scope an application's JWT to the application path. If disabled, the JWT will scope to the hostname by default |
| `read_service_tokens_from_header` | string | Allows matching Access Service Tokens passed HTTP in a single header with this name. |
| `same_site_cookie_attribute` | string | Sets the SameSite cookie setting, which provides increased security against CSRF attacks. |
| `scim_config` | json | Configuration for provisioning to this application via SCIM. This is currently in closed beta. |
| `service_auth_401_redirect` | boolean | Returns a 401 status code when the request is blocked by a Service Auth policy. |
| `skip_interstitial` | boolean | Enables automatic authentication through cloudflared. |
| `use_clientless_isolation_app_launcher_url` | boolean | Determines if users can access this application via a clientless browser isolation URL. This allows users to access private domains without connecting to Gateway. The option requires Clientless Browser Isolation to be set up with policies that allow users of this application. |
| `saas_app` | json | saas app |
| `app_launcher_logo_url` | string | The image URL of the logo shown in the App Launcher header. |
| `bg_color` | string | The background color of the App Launcher page. |
| `footer_links` | json | The links in the App Launcher footer. |
| `header_bg_color` | string | The background color of the App Launcher header. |
| `landing_page_design` | json | The design of the App Launcher landing page shown to users when they log in. |
| `skip_app_launcher_login_page` | boolean | Determines when to skip the App Launcher landing page. |
| `target_criteria` | json | target criteria |
| `is_default` | json | Rules evaluated with an AND logical operator. To match a policy, a user must meet all of the Require rules. |
| `approval_groups` | json | Administrators who can approve a temporary authentication request. |
| `connection_rules` | json | The rules that define how users may connect to the targets secured by your application. |
| `private_routing` | boolean | Enables private network routing to the origin. |
| `metadata` | json | Metadata associated with the tunnel. |
| `pattern` | string | Pattern to match incoming requests against. \[Learn more\]\(https://developers.cloudflare.com/workers/configuration/routing/routes/#matching-behavior). |
| `script` | string | Name of the script to run if the route matches. |
| `cname_suffix` | string | Allows the customer to use a custom apex. *Tenants Only Configuration*. |
| `original_dnshost` | string | DNS host at the time of switching to Cloudflare. |
| `original_registrar` | string | Registrar for the domain at the time of switching to Cloudflare. |
| `tenant` | json | The root organizational unit that this zone belongs to \(such as a tenant or organization\). |
| `tenant_unit` | json | The immediate parent organizational unit that this zone belongs to \(such as under a tenant or sub-organization\). |
| `verification_key` | string | Verification key for partial zone setup. |
| `success` | boolean | Whether Cloudflare accepted the operation |
| `leak_report_url` | string | Where the leaked `client_secret` was found, if provided by the reporter. Omitted if the token has not been reported as leaked. |
| `leak_reported_at` | string | When a secret scanning partner reported this service token's `client_secret` as leaked. A leaked service token is disabled and must be rotated before it can be re-enabled. Cleared once the secret is rotated. Omitted if the token has not been reported as leaked. |
| `leak_reporter` | string | The secret scanning partner that reported the leak. Omitted if the token has not been reported as leaked. |
| `token` | string | Token for connecting cloudflared to the tunnel |
| `annotations` | json | Annotations for the Worker version. Annotations are not inherited across settings updates; omitting this field means the new version will have no annotations. |
| `cache_options` | json | Global CacheW configuration for the Worker. When caching is on, the platform provisions a `cloudflare.app` zone for the Worker. A `type: worker` entry in the `exports` map can override this value for a single entrypoint. |
| `exports` | json | Declarative exports for the Worker, keyed by export name. Worker entrypoint entries \(`type: worker`\) carry cache configuration for that entrypoint. Durable Object entries \(`type: durable-object`\) are also configured here and drive namespace reconciliation. At most 100 entries; class names are at most 128 characters. |
| `exports_reconciliation` | json | Summary of what the declarative exports reconciliation did on this upload. |
| `result_info` | json | Pagination metadata returned by Cloudflare |

### Cloudflare Get Zone Settings

Reads zone settings such as SSL mode, minimum TLS version, security level, and caching level. Cloudflare retired the endpoint that read every setting in one request, so each setting is read individually — name the ones you need to keep the read small. Defaults to ssl, always_use_https, min_tls_version, tls_1_3, security_level, cache_level, browser_cache_ttl, development_mode, rocket_loader, email_obfuscation, hotlink_protection, ip_geolocation, http2, http3, websockets.

#### Input

| Parameter | Type | Required | Description |
| --------- | ---- | -------- | ----------- |
| `zoneId` | string | Yes | The zone ID to get settings for |
| `settingIds` | string | No | Comma-separated setting IDs to read, e.g. "ssl,min_tls_version,security_level". Leave blank to read the default set \(ssl, always_use_https, min_tls_version, tls_1_3, security_level, cache_level, browser_cache_ttl, development_mode, rocket_loader, email_obfuscation, hotlink_protection, ip_geolocation, http2, http3, websockets\). At most 40 settings per call. |
| `apiKey` | string | Yes | Cloudflare API Token |

#### Output

| Parameter | Type | Description |
| --------- | ---- | ----------- |
| `settings` | array | The zone settings that were readable |
|   ↳ `id` | string | Setting identifier \(e.g., ssl, cache_level, security_level, always_use_https\) |
|   ↳ `value` | string | Setting value as a string. Simple values returned as-is \(e.g., "full", "on"\). Complex values are JSON-stringified \(e.g., \{"css":"on","html":"on","js":"on"\}\). |
|   ↳ `editable` | boolean | Whether the setting can be modified for the current zone plan |
|   ↳ `modified_on` | string | ISO 8601 timestamp when the setting was last modified |
|   ↳ `time_remaining` | number | Development mode countdown, in seconds. Cloudflare documents this only on the zones_development_mode setting, where it is the interval from when development mode expires \(positive\) or last expired \(negative\) |
| `unreadable` | array | Requested settings Cloudflare refused, typically because the zone plan does not expose them or the setting ID does not exist |
|   ↳ `id` | string | The requested setting identifier |
|   ↳ `error` | string | Why Cloudflare would not return the setting |

### Cloudflare List Access Applications

Lists the Cloudflare Access (Zero Trust) applications protecting an account. Requires an API token with Account Access: Apps and Policies Read.

#### Input

| Parameter | Type | Required | Description |
| --------- | ---- | -------- | ----------- |
| `target_attributes` | string | No | Target Criteria attributes in key=value format. |
| `accountId` | string | Yes | The Cloudflare account ID. Access applications are account-scoped |
| `name` | string | No | Filter by application name |
| `domain` | string | No | Filter by the primary hostname the application secures |
| `aud` | string | No | Filter by application audience \(AUD\) tag |
| `search` | string | No | Free-text search across applications |
| `exact` | boolean | No | Whether the name and domain filters must match exactly |
| `page` | number | No | Page number for pagination |
| `per_page` | number | No | Number of applications per page |
| `apiKey` | string | Yes | Cloudflare API Token |

#### Output

| Parameter | Type | Description |
| --------- | ---- | ----------- |
| `applications` | array | Applications |
| `total_count` | number | Total matching resources, or page count when the API does not supply a total |
| `result_info` | json | Pagination metadata returned by Cloudflare |

### Cloudflare List Access Groups

Lists the reusable Cloudflare Access (Zero Trust) groups in an account. Groups bundle identity rules that policies can reference by ID. Requires an API token with Account Access: Organizations, Identity Providers, and Groups Read.

#### Input

| Parameter | Type | Required | Description |
| --------- | ---- | -------- | ----------- |
| `accountId` | string | Yes | The Cloudflare account ID. Access groups are account-scoped |
| `name` | string | No | Filter by group name |
| `search` | string | No | Free-text search across groups |
| `page` | number | No | Page number for pagination |
| `per_page` | number | No | Number of groups per page |
| `apiKey` | string | Yes | Cloudflare API Token |

#### Output

| Parameter | Type | Description |
| --------- | ---- | ----------- |
| `groups` | array | Groups |
| `total_count` | number | Total matching resources, or page count when the API does not supply a total |
| `result_info` | json | Pagination metadata returned by Cloudflare |

### Cloudflare List Access Identity Providers

Lists the identity providers configured for Cloudflare Access (Zero Trust) in an account, such as Okta, Entra ID, Google Workspace, or a one-time PIN. Use the returned IDs to restrict an application with allowed_idps. Requires an API token with Account Access: Organizations, Identity Providers, and Groups Read.

#### Input

| Parameter | Type | Required | Description |
| --------- | ---- | -------- | ----------- |
| `scim_enabled` | string | No | Indicates to Access to only retrieve identity providers that have the System for Cross-Domain Identity Management \(SCIM\) enabled. |
| `page` | number | No | Page number of results. |
| `per_page` | number | No | Number of results per page. |
| `accountId` | string | Yes | The Cloudflare account ID. Identity providers are account-scoped |
| `apiKey` | string | Yes | Cloudflare API Token |

#### Output

| Parameter | Type | Description |
| --------- | ---- | ----------- |
| `identity_providers` | array | Identity providers |
| `total_count` | number | Total matching resources, or page count when the API does not supply a total |
| `result_info` | json | Pagination metadata returned by Cloudflare |

### Cloudflare List Access Policies

Lists the Cloudflare Access (Zero Trust) policies attached to an application, in precedence order. Requires an API token with Account Access: Apps and Policies Read.

### Cloudflare List Access Service Tokens

Lists the Cloudflare Access (Zero Trust) service tokens in an account, which let machines authenticate to Access-protected applications. Client secrets are never returned by this endpoint — only on creation. Requires an API token with Account Access: Service Tokens Read.

| `search` | string | No | Free-text search across service tokens |
| `page` | number | No | Page number for pagination |
| `per_page` | number | No | Number of service tokens per page |
| `apiKey` | string | Yes | Cloudflare API Token |

| Parameter | Type | Description |
| --------- | ---- | ----------- |
| `service_tokens` | array | Service tokens |
| `total_count` | number | Total matching resources, or page count when the API does not supply a total |
| `result_info` | json | Pagination metadata returned by Cloudflare |

| `page` | number | No | Page number of paginated results \(default: 1\) |

### Cloudflare List DNS Records

Lists DNS records for a specific zone.

#### Input

| Parameter | Type | Required | Description |
| --------- | ---- | -------- | ----------- |
| `name_contains` | string | No | Substring of the DNS record name. Name filters are case-insensitive. |
| `name_startswith` | string | No | Prefix of the DNS record name. Name filters are case-insensitive. |
| `name_endswith` | string | No | Suffix of the DNS record name. Name filters are case-insensitive. |
| `content_contains` | string | No | Substring of the DNS record content. Content filters are case-insensitive. |
| `content_startswith` | string | No | Prefix of the DNS record content. Content filters are case-insensitive. |
| `content_endswith` | string | No | Suffix of the DNS record content. Content filters are case-insensitive. |
| `comment_present` | boolean | No | If this parameter is present, only records *with* a comment are returned. |
| `comment_absent` | boolean | No | If this parameter is present, only records *without* a comment are returned. |
| `comment_exact` | string | No | Exact value of the DNS record comment. Comment filters are case-insensitive. |
| `comment_startswith` | string | No | Prefix of the DNS record comment. Comment filters are case-insensitive. |
| `comment_endswith` | string | No | Suffix of the DNS record comment. Comment filters are case-insensitive. |
| `tag_present` | string | No | Name of a tag which must be present on the DNS record. Tag filters are case-insensitive. |
| `tag_absent` | string | No | Name of a tag which must *not* be present on the DNS record. Tag filters are case-insensitive. |
| `tag_contains` | string | No | A tag and value, of the form `&lt;tag-name&gt;:&lt;tag-value&gt;`. The API will only return DNS records that have a tag named `&lt;tag-name&gt;` whose value contains `&lt;tag-value&gt;`. Tag filters are case-insensitive. |
| `tag_startswith` | string | No | A tag and value, of the form `&lt;tag-name&gt;:&lt;tag-value&gt;`. The API will only return DNS records that have a tag named `&lt;tag-name&gt;` whose value starts with `&lt;tag-value&gt;`. Tag filters are case-insensitive. |
| `tag_endswith` | string | No | A tag and value, of the form `&lt;tag-name&gt;:&lt;tag-value&gt;`. The API will only return DNS records that have a tag named `&lt;tag-name&gt;` whose value ends with `&lt;tag-value&gt;`. Tag filters are case-insensitive. |
| `include_shadow_metadata` | boolean | No | Whether to include shadow metadata in the `meta` field of each record in the response. See \[Shadowed records\]\(https://developers.cloudflare.com/dns/manage-dns-records/reference/shadowed-records). |
| `shadowed_by_name` | string | No | Filters the response to records at or below the specified NS delegation name. NS, DS, and NSEC records at the delegation name are excluded because they are not shadowed by that delegation. Those record types are included only when they exist below the delegation. The value must be a non-apex subdomain of the zone. Requires `include_shadow_metadata=true`. See \[Shadowed records\]\(https://developers.cloudflare.com/dns/manage-dns-records/reference/shadowed-records). |
| `shadowing_name` | string | No | Returns NS records that shadow the given name, searching at the name itself and each of its ancestor names within the zone, excluding the zone apex. The value must be a subdomain of the zone; the zone apex is not accepted. See \[Shadowed records\]\(https://developers.cloudflare.com/dns/manage-dns-records/reference/shadowed-records). |
| `zoneId` | string | Yes | The zone ID to list DNS records for |
| `type` | string | No | Filter by record type \(e.g., "A", "AAAA", "CNAME", "MX", "TXT"\) |
| `name` | string | No | Filter by record name \(exact match\) |
| `content` | string | No | Filter by record content \(exact match\) |
| `page` | number | No | Page number for pagination \(default: 1\) |
| `per_page` | number | No | Number of records per page \(default: 100, max: 5000000\) |
| `direction` | string | No | Sort direction \(asc or desc\) |
| `match` | string | No | Match logic for filters: any or all \(default: all\) |
| `order` | string | No | Sort field \(type, name, content, ttl, proxied\) |
| `proxied` | boolean | No | Filter by proxy status |
| `search` | string | No | Free-text search across record name, content, and value |
| `tag` | string | No | Filter by an exact tag name |
| `tag_match` | string | No | Tag filter match logic: any or all. Only affects results when combined with multiple tag filter conditions; has no effect with the single exact-match Tag Filter above. |
| `commentFilter` | string | No | Filter records by comment content \(substring match\) |
| `apiKey` | string | Yes | Cloudflare API Token |

#### Output

| Parameter | Type | Description |
| --------- | ---- | ----------- |
| `records` | array | Records |
| `total_count` | number | Total matching resources, or page count when the API does not supply a total |
| `result_info` | json | Pagination metadata returned by Cloudflare |

### Cloudflare List Managed Ruleset Overrides

Lists the WAF managed rulesets deployed on a zone together with the overrides applied to each one. Cloudflare has no dedicated overrides endpoint — overrides live on the "execute" rules of the http_request_firewall_managed phase entry point ruleset, which this reads. Requires an API token with Zone WAF Read.

#### Input

| Parameter | Type | Required | Description |
| --------- | ---- | -------- | ----------- |
| `zoneId` | string | Yes | The zone ID to read managed ruleset deployments and overrides for |
| `apiKey` | string | Yes | Cloudflare API Token |

#### Output

| Parameter | Type | Description |
| --------- | ---- | ----------- |
| `ruleset_id` | string | Ruleset ID of the http_request_firewall_managed entry point, needed to edit a deployment rule |
| `deployments` | array | Managed rulesets deployed on the zone and the overrides applied to each |
|   ↳ `rule_id` | string | ID of the execute rule that deploys the managed ruleset |
|   ↳ `managed_ruleset_id` | string | ID of the deployed managed ruleset |
|   ↳ `description` | string | Description of the deployment rule |
|   ↳ `expression` | string | Filter expression scoping which requests the managed ruleset runs on |
|   ↳ `enabled` | boolean | Whether the deployment is enabled |
|   ↳ `overrides` | json | Overrides applied to the managed ruleset, at three levels. Cloudflare documents action, enabled, and sensitivity_level at the ruleset \(top\) level; category, action, enabled, and sensitivity_level per category; and id, action, enabled, score_threshold, and sensitivity_level per rule. Rule overrides beat category overrides, which beat the ruleset-level override. sensitivity_level applies only to the DDoS phases, so for a WAF managed ruleset the rule-level properties are action, enabled, and score_threshold |
| `total_count` | number | Number of managed ruleset deployments found |

### Cloudflare List R2 Buckets

Lists the R2 object storage buckets in an account. Requires an API token with Account Workers R2 Storage Read.

#### Input

| Parameter | Type | Required | Description |
| --------- | ---- | -------- | ----------- |
| `order` | string | No | Order buckets by name. Allowed values: name. |
| `accountId` | string | Yes | The Cloudflare account ID. R2 buckets are account-scoped |
| `name_contains` | string | No | Only return buckets whose name contains this substring |
| `start_after` | string | No | Bucket name to start listing after |
| `cursor` | string | No | Pagination cursor returned by a previous call |
| `direction` | string | No | Sort direction by bucket name: asc or desc |
| `per_page` | number | No | Number of buckets per page |
| `jurisdiction` | string | No | Data-residency jurisdiction to list within: default, eu, or fedramp |
| `apiKey` | string | Yes | Cloudflare API Token |

#### Output

| Parameter | Type | Description |
| --------- | ---- | ----------- |
| `buckets` | array | Buckets |
| `total_count` | number | Total matching resources, or page count when the API does not supply a total |
| `result_info` | json | Pagination metadata returned by Cloudflare |
| `cursor` | string | Cursor for the next page |

### Cloudflare List Rate Limiting Rules

Lists the rate limiting rules on a zone by reading the http_ratelimit phase entry point ruleset. This uses the current Rulesets-based rate limiting API; the legacy rate_limits endpoint is no longer available. The returned ruleset ID is what "Create Rate Limiting Rule", "Update Rate Limiting Rule", and "Delete Ruleset Rule" need. Requires an API token with Zone WAF Read.

#### Input

| Parameter | Type | Required | Description |
| --------- | ---- | -------- | ----------- |
| `zoneId` | string | Yes | The zone ID to list rate limiting rules for |
| `apiKey` | string | Yes | Cloudflare API Token |

#### Output

| Parameter | Type | Description |
| --------- | ---- | ----------- |
| `zones` | json | List of zones/domains |
| `records` | json | List of DNS records |
| `certificates` | json | List of SSL/TLS certificate packs |
| `settings` | json | DNS record settings, Worker configuration, or zone settings depending on the operation |
| `unreadable` | json | Requested zone settings Cloudflare refused, with the reason for each |
| `totals` | json | Aggregate DNS analytics totals |
| `min` | json | Per-metric DNS analytics minimums. Cloudflare documents this as currently always an empty object |
| `max` | json | Per-metric DNS analytics maximums. Cloudflare documents this as currently always an empty object |
| `query` | json | Echo of the DNS analytics query parameters sent |
| `data` | json | Structured DNS record data, or DNS analytics rows depending on the operation |
| `data_lag` | number | Processing lag in seconds before analytics data becomes available |
| `rows` | number | Total number of rows in the DNS analytics result set |
| `id` | string | Resource ID |
| `name` | string | Resource name |
| `status` | string | Resource status |
| `paused` | boolean | Whether the zone is paused |
| `type` | string | Zone or record type |
| `name_servers` | json | Assigned Cloudflare name servers |
| `original_name_servers` | json | Original registrar name servers |
| `plan` | json | Zone plan information \(id, name, price, currency, frequency, is_subscribed, legacy_id\) |
| `account` | json | Account the zone belongs to \(id, name\) |

Defaults to true. Set to false to disable the eager redirect cookie behavior. |

| `use_clientless_isolation_app_launcher_url` | boolean | Determines if users can access this application via a clientless browser isolation URL. This allows users to access private domains without connecting to Gateway. The option requires Clientless Browser Isolation to be set up with policies that allow users of this application. |

| `cname_suffix` | string | Allows the customer to use a custom apex. *Tenants Only Configuration*. |

| `original_registrar` | string | Registrar for the domain at the time of switching to Cloudflare. |

| `tenant_unit` | json | The immediate parent organizational unit that this zone belongs to \(such as under a tenant or sub-organization\). |
| `verification_key` | string | Verification key for partial zone setup. |
| `success` | boolean | Whether Cloudflare accepted the operation |
| `leak_report_url` | string | Where the leaked `client_secret` was found, if provided by the reporter. Omitted if the token has not been reported as leaked. |
| `leak_reported_at` | string | When a secret scanning partner reported this service token's `client_secret` as leaked. A leaked service token is disabled and must be rotated before it can be re-enabled. Cleared once the secret is rotated. Omitted if the token has not been reported as leaked. |
| `leak_reporter` | string | The secret scanning partner that reported the leak. Omitted if the token has not been reported as leaked. |
| `token` | string | Token for connecting cloudflared to the tunnel |
| `annotations` | json | Annotations for the Worker version. Annotations are not inherited across settings updates; omitting this field means the new version will have no annotations. |
| `cache_options` | json | Global CacheW configuration for the Worker. When caching is on, the platform provisions a `cloudflare.app` zone for the Worker. A `type: worker` entry in the `exports` map can override this value for a single entrypoint. |
| `exports` | json | Declarative exports for the Worker, keyed by export name. Worker entrypoint entries \(`type: worker`\) carry cache configuration for that entrypoint. Durable Object entries \(`type: durable-object`\) are also configured here and drive namespace reconciliation. At most 100 entries; class names are at most 128 characters. |
| `exports_reconciliation` | json | Summary of what the declarative exports reconciliation did on this upload. |
| `result_info` | json | Pagination metadata returned by Cloudflare |

### Cloudflare List Rulesets

Lists every ruleset defined on a zone across all phases (WAF custom rules, managed rules, rate limiting, transform rules, and more). The list response deliberately omits the rules inside each ruleset — use "Get Ruleset" to read them. Requires an API token with Zone WAF Read (or another matching ruleset Read permission).

#### Input

| Parameter | Type | Required | Description |
| --------- | ---- | -------- | ----------- |
| `zoneId` | string | Yes | The zone ID to list rulesets for |
| `per_page` | number | No | Number of rulesets to return per page |
| `cursor` | string | No | Cursor for the next page, taken from the cursor output of a previous call. This endpoint paginates by cursor, not by page number |
| `apiKey` | string | Yes | Cloudflare API Token |

#### Output

| Parameter | Type | Description |
| --------- | ---- | ----------- |
| `rulesets` | array | Rulesets |
| `total_count` | number | Total matching resources, or page count when the API does not supply a total |
| `result_info` | json | Pagination metadata returned by Cloudflare |
| `cursor` | string | Cursor for the next page |

### Cloudflare List Tunnels

Lists the Cloudflare Tunnels (cloudflared) in an account, with their health status and active connections. Requires an API token with Account Cloudflare Tunnel Read.

#### Input

| Parameter | Type | Required | Description |
| --------- | ---- | -------- | ----------- |
| `accountId` | string | Yes | The Cloudflare account ID. Tunnels are account-scoped |
| `name` | string | No | Filter by exact tunnel name |
| `status` | string | No | Filter by tunnel health: inactive, degraded, healthy, or down |
| `uuid` | string | No | Filter by tunnel UUID |
| `is_deleted` | boolean | No | Whether to return deleted tunnels instead of active ones |
| `include_prefix` | string | No | Only include tunnels whose name starts with this prefix |
| `exclude_prefix` | string | No | Exclude tunnels whose name starts with this prefix |
| `existed_at` | string | No | Return tunnels that existed at this RFC 3339 timestamp |
| `was_active_at` | string | No | Return tunnels that were active at this RFC 3339 timestamp |
| `was_inactive_at` | string | No | Return tunnels that were inactive at this RFC 3339 timestamp |
| `page` | number | No | Page number for pagination |
| `per_page` | number | No | Number of tunnels per page |
| `apiKey` | string | Yes | Cloudflare API Token |

#### Output

| Parameter | Type | Description |
| --------- | ---- | ----------- |
| `tunnels` | array | Tunnels |
| `total_count` | number | Total matching resources, or page count when the API does not supply a total |
| `result_info` | json | Pagination metadata returned by Cloudflare |

### Cloudflare List Worker Routes

Lists the Workers routes on a zone, showing which URL patterns are handled by which Worker script. Unlike the Workers script endpoints, routes are zone-scoped. Requires an API token with Zone Workers Routes Read.

#### Input

| Parameter | Type | Required | Description |
| --------- | ---- | -------- | ----------- |
| `zoneId` | string | Yes | The zone ID to list Workers routes for. Routes are zone-scoped, not account-scoped |
| `apiKey` | string | Yes | Cloudflare API Token |

#### Output

| Parameter | Type | Description |
| --------- | ---- | ----------- |
| `routes` | array | Routes |
| `total_count` | number | Total matching resources, or page count when the API does not supply a total |
| `result_info` | json | Pagination metadata returned by Cloudflare |

### Cloudflare List Worker Scripts

Lists the Workers scripts deployed in an account. Requires an API token with Account Workers Scripts Read.

#### Input

| Parameter | Type | Required | Description |
| --------- | ---- | -------- | ----------- |
| `accountId` | string | Yes | The Cloudflare account ID. Workers scripts are account-scoped |
| `tags` | string | No | Filter scripts by tag. Cloudflare expects a comma-separated list of tag:allowed pairs where allowed is yes or no, e.g. team:core:yes,deprecated:no |
| `apiKey` | string | Yes | Cloudflare API Token |

#### Output

| Parameter | Type | Description |
| --------- | ---- | ----------- |
| `scripts` | array | Scripts |
| `total_count` | number | Total matching resources, or page count when the API does not supply a total |
| `result_info` | json | Pagination metadata returned by Cloudflare |

### Cloudflare List Zones

Lists one page of zones (domains) in the Cloudflare account.

#### Input

| Parameter | Type | Required | Description |
| --------- | ---- | -------- | ----------- |
| `type` | string | No | Comma-separated zone types: full, partial, secondary, internal |
| `account_name` | string | No | An account Name. Optional filter operators can be provided to extend refine the search: * `equal` \(default\) * `not_equal` * `starts_with` * `ends_with` * `contains` * `starts_with_case_sensitive` * `ends_with_case_sensitive` * `contains_case_sensitive` |
| `name` | string | No | Filter zones by domain name \(e.g., "example.com"\) |
| `status` | string | No | Filter by zone status: "initializing", "pending", "active", or "moved" |
| `page` | number | No | Page number for pagination \(default: 1\) |
| `per_page` | number | No | Number of zones per page \(default: 20, max: 50\) |
| `accountId` | string | No | Filter zones by account ID |
| `order` | string | No | Sort field \(name, status, account.id, account.name, plan.id\) |
| `direction` | string | No | Sort direction \(asc, desc\) |
| `match` | string | No | Match logic for filters \(any, all\). Default: all |
| `apiKey` | string | Yes | Cloudflare API Token |

#### Output

| Parameter | Type | Description |
| --------- | ---- | ----------- |
| `zones` | array | Zones |
| `total_count` | number | Total matching resources, or page count when the API does not supply a total |
| `result_info` | json | Pagination metadata returned by Cloudflare |

### Cloudflare Purge Cache

Purges cached content for a zone. Can purge everything or specific files/tags/hosts/prefixes.

#### Input

| Parameter | Type | Required | Description |
| --------- | ---- | -------- | ----------- |
| `zoneId` | string | Yes | The zone ID to purge cache for |
| `purge_everything` | boolean | No | Set to true to purge all cached content. Mutually exclusive with files, tags, hosts, and prefixes |
| `files` | string | No | Comma-separated URLs or a JSON array of URL strings and \{url, headers\} objects. Use headers for custom cache keys, for example \[\{"url":"https://example.com/a","headers":\{"CF-IPCountry":"US"\}\}] |
| `tags` | string | No | Comma-separated list of cache tags to purge |
| `hosts` | string | No | Comma-separated list of hostnames to purge |
| `prefixes` | string | No | Comma-separated list of URL prefixes to purge |
| `apiKey` | string | Yes | Cloudflare API Token |

#### Output

| Parameter | Type | Description |
| --------- | ---- | ----------- |
| `id` | string | Purge request identifier returned by Cloudflare |

### Cloudflare Refresh Access Service Token

Refreshes the expiration of a service token.

| `accountId` | string | Yes | Account id |
| `serviceTokenId` | string | Yes | Service token id |
| `apiKey` | string | Yes | Cloudflare API token |

### Cloudflare Revoke Access Service Token

Permanently deletes a Cloudflare Access (Zero Trust) service token, revoking it. Every machine or integration still presenting that client ID and secret is locked out of the Access-protected applications immediately, and the secret cannot be recovered. This cannot be undone. Requires an API token with Account Access: Service Tokens Edit.

#### Input

| Parameter | Type | Required | Description |
| --------- | ---- | -------- | ----------- |
| `accountId` | string | Yes | The Cloudflare account ID. Service tokens are account-scoped |
| `serviceTokenId` | string | Yes | The service token ID to revoke permanently |
| `apiKey` | string | Yes | Cloudflare API Token |

#### Output

| Parameter | Type | Description |
| --------- | ---- | ----------- |
| `zones` | json | List of zones/domains |
| `records` | json | List of DNS records |
| `certificates` | json | List of SSL/TLS certificate packs |
| `settings` | json | DNS record settings, Worker configuration, or zone settings depending on the operation |
| `unreadable` | json | Requested zone settings Cloudflare refused, with the reason for each |
| `totals` | json | Aggregate DNS analytics totals |
| `min` | json | Per-metric DNS analytics minimums. Cloudflare documents this as currently always an empty object |
| `max` | json | Per-metric DNS analytics maximums. Cloudflare documents this as currently always an empty object |
| `query` | json | Echo of the DNS analytics query parameters sent |
| `data` | json | Structured DNS record data, or DNS analytics rows depending on the operation |
| `data_lag` | number | Processing lag in seconds before analytics data becomes available |
| `rows` | number | Total number of rows in the DNS analytics result set |

### Cloudflare Rotate Access Service Token

Generates a new Client Secret for a service token and revokes the old one.

#### Input

| Parameter | Type | Required | Description |
| --------- | ---- | -------- | ----------- |
| `accountId` | string | Yes | Account id |
| `serviceTokenId` | string | Yes | Service token id |
| `apiKey` | string | Yes | Cloudflare API token |
| `previous_client_secret_expires_at` | string | No | The expiration of the previous `client_secret`. If not provided, it defaults to the current timestamp in order to immediately expire the previous secret. If the service token was reported as leaked, this must not be in the future; the leaked secret is discarded immediately. |

#### Output

| `zones` | json | List of zones/domains |

| `same_site_cookie_attribute` | string | Sets the SameSite cookie setting, which provides increased security against CSRF attacks. |
| `scim_config` | json | Configuration for provisioning to this application via SCIM. This is currently in closed beta. |
| `service_auth_401_redirect` | boolean | Returns a 401 status code when the request is blocked by a Service Auth policy. |
| `skip_interstitial` | boolean | Enables automatic authentication through cloudflared. |
| `use_clientless_isolation_app_launcher_url` | boolean | Determines if users can access this application via a clientless browser isolation URL. This allows users to access private domains without connecting to Gateway. The option requires Clientless Browser Isolation to be set up with policies that allow users of this application. |
| `saas_app` | json | saas app |
| `app_launcher_logo_url` | string | The image URL of the logo shown in the App Launcher header. |
| `bg_color` | string | The background color of the App Launcher page. |
| `footer_links` | json | The links in the App Launcher footer. |
| `header_bg_color` | string | The background color of the App Launcher header. |
| `landing_page_design` | json | The design of the App Launcher landing page shown to users when they log in. |
| `skip_app_launcher_login_page` | boolean | Determines when to skip the App Launcher landing page. |
| `target_criteria` | json | target criteria |
| `is_default` | json | Rules evaluated with an AND logical operator. To match a policy, a user must meet all of the Require rules. |
| `approval_groups` | json | Administrators who can approve a temporary authentication request. |
| `connection_rules` | json | The rules that define how users may connect to the targets secured by your application. |
| `private_routing` | boolean | Enables private network routing to the origin. |
| `metadata` | json | Metadata associated with the tunnel. |
| `pattern` | string | Pattern to match incoming requests against. \[Learn more\]\(https://developers.cloudflare.com/workers/configuration/routing/routes/#matching-behavior). |
| `script` | string | Name of the script to run if the route matches. |
| `cname_suffix` | string | Allows the customer to use a custom apex. *Tenants Only Configuration*. |
| `original_dnshost` | string | DNS host at the time of switching to Cloudflare. |
| `original_registrar` | string | Registrar for the domain at the time of switching to Cloudflare. |
| `tenant` | json | The root organizational unit that this zone belongs to \(such as a tenant or organization\). |
| `tenant_unit` | json | The immediate parent organizational unit that this zone belongs to \(such as under a tenant or sub-organization\). |
| `verification_key` | string | Verification key for partial zone setup. |
| `success` | boolean | Whether Cloudflare accepted the operation |
| `leak_report_url` | string | Where the leaked `client_secret` was found, if provided by the reporter. Omitted if the token has not been reported as leaked. |
| `leak_reported_at` | string | When a secret scanning partner reported this service token's `client_secret` as leaked. A leaked service token is disabled and must be rotated before it can be re-enabled. Cleared once the secret is rotated. Omitted if the token has not been reported as leaked. |
| `leak_reporter` | string | The secret scanning partner that reported the leak. Omitted if the token has not been reported as leaked. |
| `token` | string | Token for connecting cloudflared to the tunnel |
| `annotations` | json | Annotations for the Worker version. Annotations are not inherited across settings updates; omitting this field means the new version will have no annotations. |
| `cache_options` | json | Global CacheW configuration for the Worker. When caching is on, the platform provisions a `cloudflare.app` zone for the Worker. A `type: worker` entry in the `exports` map can override this value for a single entrypoint. |
| `exports` | json | Declarative exports for the Worker, keyed by export name. Worker entrypoint entries \(`type: worker`\) carry cache configuration for that entrypoint. Durable Object entries \(`type: durable-object`\) are also configured here and drive namespace reconciliation. At most 100 entries; class names are at most 128 characters. |
| `exports_reconciliation` | json | Summary of what the declarative exports reconciliation did on this upload. |
| `result_info` | json | Pagination metadata returned by Cloudflare |

### Cloudflare Update Access Application

Updates a Cloudflare Access (Zero Trust) application. Cloudflare does not document merge behavior for this PUT, so treat it as a replace: send every field the application should keep, because an omitted field may revert to its default and widen or break access. Read the current configuration with "Get Access Application" first. Requires an API token with Account Access: Apps and Policies Edit.

#### Input

| Parameter | Type | Required | Description |
| --------- | ---- | -------- | ----------- |
| `destinations` | json | No | JSON array. List of destinations secured by Access. This supersedes `self_hosted_domains` to allow for more flexibility in defining different types of domains. If `destinations` are provided, then `self_hosted_domains` will be ignored. Fields: overrides, type, uri, cidr, hostname, l4_protocol, port_range, vnet_id, mcp_server_id, worker_id. |
| `oauth_configuration` | json | No | JSON object. Required for end_user applications. **Beta:** Optional configuration for managing an OAuth authorization flow controlled by Access. When set, Access will act as the OAuth authorization server for this application. Only compatible with OAuth clients that support \[RFC 8707\]\(https://datatracker.ietf.org/doc/html/rfc8707) \(Resource Indicators for OAuth 2.0\). This feature is currently in beta. Fields: dynamic_client_registration, enabled, grant. |
| `self_hosted_domains` | json | No | JSON array. List of public domains that Access will secure. This field is deprecated in favor of `destinations` and will be supported until **November 21, 2025.** If `destinations` are provided, then `self_hosted_domains` will be ignored. |
| `user_populations` | json | No | JSON array. Required for end_user applications. The single user population associated with this application. |
| `allow_authenticate_via_warp` | boolean | No | When set to true, users can authenticate to this application using their WARP session. When set to false this application will always require direct IdP authentication. This setting always overrides the organization setting for WARP authentication. |
| `allow_iframe` | boolean | No | Enables loading application content in an iFrame. |
| `cors_headers` | json | No | JSON object. cors headers Fields: allow_all_headers, allow_all_methods, allow_all_origins, allow_credentials, allowed_headers, allowed_methods, allowed_origins, max_age. |
| `custom_non_identity_deny_url` | string | No | The custom URL a user is redirected to when they are denied access to the application when failing non-identity rules. |
| `custom_pages` | json | No | JSON array. The custom pages that will be displayed when applicable for this application |
| `eager_redirect_cookie_setting` | boolean | No | Preemptively sets the Access session cookie on every hostname in a multi-hostname self-hosted application during the initial redirect chain, rather than setting it lazily on first visit. Defaults to true. Set to false to disable the eager redirect cookie behavior. |
| `enable_binding_cookie` | boolean | No | Enables the binding cookie, which increases security against compromised authorization tokens and CSRF attacks. |
| `http_only_cookie_attribute` | boolean | No | Enables the HttpOnly cookie attribute, which increases security against XSS attacks. |
| `mfa_config` | json | No | JSON object. Configures multi-factor authentication \(MFA\) settings. Fields: allowed_authenticators, mfa_disabled, session_duration. |
| `options_preflight_bypass` | boolean | No | Allows options preflight requests to bypass Access authentication and go directly to the origin. Cannot turn on if cors_headers is set. |
| `path_cookie_attribute` | boolean | No | Enables cookie paths to scope an application's JWT to the application path. If disabled, the JWT will scope to the hostname by default |
| `read_service_tokens_from_header` | string | No | Allows matching Access Service Tokens passed HTTP in a single header with this name. This works as an alternative to the \(CF-Access-Client-Id, CF-Access-Client-Secret\) pair of headers. The header value will be interpreted as a json object similar to: \{ "cf-access-client-id": "&lt;CLIENT_ID&gt;", "cf-access-client-secret": "&lt;CLIENT_SECRET&gt;" \} |
| `same_site_cookie_attribute` | string | No | Sets the SameSite cookie setting, which provides increased security against CSRF attacks. |
| `scim_config` | json | No | JSON object. Configuration for provisioning to this application via SCIM. This is currently in closed beta. Fields: authentication, deactivate_on_delete, enabled, idp_uid, mappings, remote_uri. |
| `service_auth_401_redirect` | boolean | No | Returns a 401 status code when the request is blocked by a Service Auth policy. |
| `skip_interstitial` | boolean | No | Enables automatic authentication through cloudflared. |
| `use_clientless_isolation_app_launcher_url` | boolean | No | Determines if users can access this application via a clientless browser isolation URL. This allows users to access private domains without connecting to Gateway. The option requires Clientless Browser Isolation to be set up with policies that allow users of this application. |
| `app_launcher_logo_url` | string | No | The image URL of the logo shown in the App Launcher header. |
| `bg_color` | string | No | The background color of the App Launcher page. |
| `footer_links` | json | No | JSON array. The links in the App Launcher footer. Fields: name, url. |
| `header_bg_color` | string | No | The background color of the App Launcher header. |
| `landing_page_design` | json | No | JSON object. The design of the App Launcher landing page shown to users when they log in. Fields: button_color, button_text_color, image_url, message, title. |
| `skip_app_launcher_login_page` | boolean | No | Determines when to skip the App Launcher landing page. |
| `accountId` | string | Yes | The Cloudflare account ID. Access applications are account-scoped |
| `appId` | string | Yes | The Access application ID to update |
| `type` | string | Yes | Application type: self_hosted, end_user, saas, ssh, vnc, app_launcher, warp, biso, bookmark, infrastructure, rdp, mcp, mcp_portal, or proxy_endpoint. dash_sso has no request variant and cannot be written through the API |
| `domain` | string | No | The primary hostname and path secured by Access. Required for the self_hosted, ssh, vnc, and rdp types; optional for bookmark and mcp_portal; read-only for app_launcher, warp, biso, and proxy_endpoint; and absent from the saas, infrastructure, and mcp variants |
| `name` | string | No | Friendly name shown in the dashboard and App Launcher |
| `sessionDuration` | string | No | How long an Access session stays valid, e.g. 24h or 30m |
| `allowedIdps` | string | No | Comma-separated identity provider IDs users may authenticate with |
| `appLauncherVisible` | boolean | No | Whether the application is shown in the App Launcher |
| `autoRedirectToIdentity` | boolean | No | Whether users skip the identity provider picker |
| `customDenyMessage` | string | No | Message shown to users who are denied access |
| `customDenyUrl` | string | No | URL denied users are redirected to |
| `logoUrl` | string | No | Logo image URL shown in the dashboard and App Launcher |
| `tags` | string | No | Comma-separated tag names categorizing the application |
| `saasApp` | string | No | JSON SaaS configuration, required for the saas type and rejected on every other type. SAML, e.g. \{ "auth_type": "saml", "consumer_service_url": "https://example.com/acs", "sp_entity_id": "https://example.com" \}; OIDC, e.g. \{ "auth_type": "oidc", "client_id": "...", "redirect_uris": \[ "https://example.com/callback" \] \} |
| `targetCriteria` | string | No | JSON array of infrastructure target criteria, required for the infrastructure and rdp types and rejected on every other type, e.g. \[\{"port":22,"protocol":"SSH","target_attributes":\{"hostname":\["production"\]\}\}\] |
| `policies` | string | No | JSON array of policies to attach. Entries may be reusable policy IDs or inline policy objects |
| `apiKey` | string | Yes | Cloudflare API Token |

### Cloudflare Update Access Group

Updates a configured Access group. Replaces the configuration; resend all values to retain.

#### Input

| Parameter | Type | Required | Description |
| --------- | ---- | -------- | ----------- |
| `accountId` | string | Yes | Account id |
| `groupId` | string | Yes | Group id |
| `apiKey` | string | Yes | Cloudflare API token |
| `exclude` | json | No | JSON array. Rules evaluated with a NOT logical operator. To match a policy, a user cannot meet any of the Exclude rules. Fields: group, any_valid_service_token, auth_context, auth_method, azureAD, certificate, common_name, geo, device_posture, email_domain, email_list, email, everyone, external_evaluation, github-organization, gsuite, login_method, ip_list, ip, okta, saml, oidc, service_token, linked_app_token, user_risk_score, cloudflare_account_member. |
| `include` | json | Yes | JSON array. Rules evaluated with an OR logical operator. A user needs to meet only one of the Include rules. Fields: group, any_valid_service_token, auth_context, auth_method, azureAD, certificate, common_name, geo, device_posture, email_domain, email_list, email, everyone, external_evaluation, github-organization, gsuite, login_method, ip_list, ip, okta, saml, oidc, service_token, linked_app_token, user_risk_score, cloudflare_account_member. |
| `is_default` | boolean | No | Whether this is the default group |
| `name` | string | Yes | The name of the Access group. |
| `require` | json | No | JSON array. Rules evaluated with an AND logical operator. To match a policy, a user must meet all of the Require rules. Fields: group, any_valid_service_token, auth_context, auth_method, azureAD, certificate, common_name, geo, device_posture, email_domain, email_list, email, everyone, external_evaluation, github-organization, gsuite, login_method, ip_list, ip, okta, saml, oidc, service_token, linked_app_token, user_risk_score, cloudflare_account_member. |

#### Output

| Parameter | Type | Description |
| --------- | ---- | ----------- |
| `zones` | json | List of zones/domains |
| `records` | json | List of DNS records |
| `certificates` | json | List of SSL/TLS certificate packs |
| `settings` | json | DNS record settings, Worker configuration, or zone settings depending on the operation |
| `unreadable` | json | Requested zone settings Cloudflare refused, with the reason for each |
| `totals` | json | Aggregate DNS analytics totals |
| `min` | json | Per-metric DNS analytics minimums. Cloudflare documents this as currently always an empty object |
| `max` | json | Per-metric DNS analytics maximums. Cloudflare documents this as currently always an empty object |
| `query` | json | Echo of the DNS analytics query parameters sent |
| `data` | json | Structured DNS record data, or DNS analytics rows depending on the operation |
| `data_lag` | number | Processing lag in seconds before analytics data becomes available |
| `rows` | number | Total number of rows in the DNS analytics result set |
| `id` | string | Resource ID |
| `name` | string | Resource name |

### Cloudflare Update Access Policy

Updates a Cloudflare Access (Zero Trust) policy on an application. Cloudflare does not document merge behavior for this PUT, so treat it as a replace: send every rule the policy should keep, because an omitted exclude or require rule may be dropped and widen who gets in. The change applies to live traffic immediately. Read the current policy with "List Access Policies" first. Requires an API token with Account Access: Apps and Policies Edit.

#### Input

| Parameter | Type | Required | Description |
| --------- | ---- | -------- | ----------- |
| `approval_groups` | json | No | JSON array. Administrators who can approve a temporary authentication request. Fields: approvals_needed, email_addresses, email_list_uuid. |
| `connection_rules` | json | No | JSON object. The rules that define how users may connect to the targets secured by your application. Fields: rdp, ssh. |
| `mfa_config` | json | No | JSON object. Configures multi-factor authentication \(MFA\) settings for infrastructure applications. Fields: allowed_authenticators, mfa_disabled, session_duration. |
| `accountId` | string | Yes | The Cloudflare account ID. Access applications are account-scoped |
| `appId` | string | Yes | The Access application ID that owns the policy |
| `policyId` | string | Yes | The Access policy ID to update |
| `name` | string | Yes | Name of the policy |
| `decision` | string | Yes | What the policy does when it matches: allow, deny, non_identity, or bypass \(skip Access entirely\) |
| `include` | string | Yes | JSON array of Access rules evaluated with OR logic. Example: \[\{"email_domain":\{"domain":"example.com"\}\}\] |
| `exclude` | string | No | JSON array of Access rules evaluated with NOT logic |
| `require` | string | No | JSON array of Access rules evaluated with AND logic |
| `precedence` | number | No | Evaluation order of the policy within the application |
| `sessionDuration` | string | No | How long a session granted by this policy stays valid, e.g. 24h. Leave it unset on a policy attached to an infrastructure-typed application — Cloudflare rejects those with error 12130 |
| `approvalRequired` | boolean | No | Whether an approver must grant each access request |
| `isolationRequired` | boolean | No | Whether the session must run in a remote isolated browser |
| `purposeJustificationRequired` | boolean | No | Whether users must state a reason for access |
| `purposeJustificationPrompt` | string | No | Prompt shown when a justification is required |
| `apiKey` | string | Yes | Cloudflare API Token |

### Cloudflare Update Access Service Token

Updates a configured service token. Replaces the configuration; resend all values to retain.

#### Input

| Parameter | Type | Required | Description |
| --------- | ---- | -------- | ----------- |
| `accountId` | string | Yes | Account id |
| `serviceTokenId` | string | Yes | Service token id |
| `apiKey` | string | Yes | Cloudflare API token |
| `client_secret_version` | number | No | A version number identifying the current `client_secret` associated with the service token. Incrementing it triggers a rotation; the previous secret will still be accepted until the time indicated by `previous_client_secret_expires_at`. If the service token was reported as leaked, `previous_client_secret_expires_at` is not required, may not be in the future, and the previous secret is discarded immediately. |
| `duration` | string | No | The duration for how long the service token will be valid. Must be in the format `300ms` or `2h45m`, or the special value `forever` for non-expiring tokens. Valid time units are: ns, us \(or µs\), ms, s, m, h. The default is 1 year in hours \(8760h\). |
| `enabled` | boolean | No | Whether the service token is enabled. A disabled service token cannot be used to authenticate; both its current and previous `client_secret` stop being accepted, but the token itself is preserved and can be re-enabled. A service token reported as leaked is disabled automatically and cannot be re-enabled until its secret is rotated; attempting to do so returns a 409. Defaults to enabled when omitted on create. |
| `name` | string | No | The name of the service token. |
| `previous_client_secret_expires_at` | string | No | The expiration of the previous `client_secret`. This can be modified at any point after a rotation. For example, you may extend it further into the future if you need more time to update services with the new secret; or move it into the past to immediately invalidate the previous token in case of compromise. Rotating a service token that was reported as leaked discards the previous secret entirely, so no grace period can be set. |

| `leak_report_url` | string | Where the leaked `client_secret` was found, if provided by the reporter. Omitted if the token has not been reported as leaked. |
| `leak_reported_at` | string | When a secret scanning partner reported this service token's `client_secret` as leaked. A leaked service token is disabled and must be rotated before it can be re-enabled. Cleared once the secret is rotated. Omitted if the token has not been reported as leaked. |
| `leak_reporter` | string | The secret scanning partner that reported the leak. Omitted if the token has not been reported as leaked. |
| `token` | string | Token for connecting cloudflared to the tunnel |
| `annotations` | json | Annotations for the Worker version. Annotations are not inherited across settings updates; omitting this field means the new version will have no annotations. |
| `cache_options` | json | Global CacheW configuration for the Worker. When caching is on, the platform provisions a `cloudflare.app` zone for the Worker. A `type: worker` entry in the `exports` map can override this value for a single entrypoint. |
| `exports` | json | Declarative exports for the Worker, keyed by export name. Worker entrypoint entries \(`type: worker`\) carry cache configuration for that entrypoint. Durable Object entries \(`type: durable-object`\) are also configured here and drive namespace reconciliation. At most 100 entries; class names are at most 128 characters. |
| `exports_reconciliation` | json | Summary of what the declarative exports reconciliation did on this upload. |
| `result_info` | json | Pagination metadata returned by Cloudflare |

### Cloudflare Update DNS Record

Updates an existing DNS record for a zone.

#### Input

| Parameter | Type | Required | Description |
| --------- | ---- | -------- | ----------- |
| `clearComment` | boolean | No | Clear the stored DNS record comment |
| `settings` | json | No | JSON object. Settings for the DNS record. Fields: ipv4_only, ipv6_only, flatten_cname. |
| `private_routing` | boolean | No | Enables private network routing to the origin. |
| `data` | json | No | JSON object. Structured record data. The available fields depend on the DNS record type. Fields: flags, tag, value, algorithm, certificate, key_tag, type, protocol, public_key, digest, digest_type, priority, target, altitude, lat_degrees, lat_direction, lat_minutes, lat_seconds, long_degrees, long_direction, long_minutes, long_seconds, precision_horz, precision_vert, size, order, preference, regex, replacement, service, matching_type, selector, usage, port, weight, fingerprint. |
| `include_shadow_metadata` | boolean | No | Whether to include shadow metadata in the `meta` field of each record in the response. See \[Shadowed records\]\(https://developers.cloudflare.com/dns/manage-dns-records/reference/shadowed-records). |
| `zoneId` | string | Yes | The zone ID containing the DNS record |
| `recordId` | string | Yes | The DNS record ID to update |
| `type` | string | No | DNS record type \(e.g., "A", "AAAA", "CNAME", "MX", "TXT"\) |
| `name` | string | No | DNS record name |
| `content` | string | No | DNS record content \(e.g., IP address\) |
| `ttl` | number | No | Time to live in seconds \(1 = automatic\) |
| `proxied` | boolean | No | Whether to enable Cloudflare proxy |
| `priority` | number | No | Record priority. Cloudflare accepts this top-level field for MX and URI records only; use the data object for structured records such as SRV \(priority, weight, port, target\) |
| `comment` | string | No | Comment for the DNS record |
| `tags` | string | No | Comma-separated tags for the DNS record |
| `apiKey` | string | Yes | Cloudflare API Token |

| `original_dnshost` | string | DNS host at the time of switching to Cloudflare. |
| `original_registrar` | string | Registrar for the domain at the time of switching to Cloudflare. |
| `tenant` | json | The root organizational unit that this zone belongs to \(such as a tenant or organization\). |
| `tenant_unit` | json | The immediate parent organizational unit that this zone belongs to \(such as under a tenant or sub-organization\). |
| `verification_key` | string | Verification key for partial zone setup. |
| `success` | boolean | Whether Cloudflare accepted the operation |
| `leak_report_url` | string | Where the leaked `client_secret` was found, if provided by the reporter. Omitted if the token has not been reported as leaked. |
| `leak_reported_at` | string | When a secret scanning partner reported this service token's `client_secret` as leaked. A leaked service token is disabled and must be rotated before it can be re-enabled. Cleared once the secret is rotated. Omitted if the token has not been reported as leaked. |
| `leak_reporter` | string | The secret scanning partner that reported the leak. Omitted if the token has not been reported as leaked. |
| `token` | string | Token for connecting cloudflared to the tunnel |
| `annotations` | json | Annotations for the Worker version. Annotations are not inherited across settings updates; omitting this field means the new version will have no annotations. |
| `cache_options` | json | Global CacheW configuration for the Worker. When caching is on, the platform provisions a `cloudflare.app` zone for the Worker. A `type: worker` entry in the `exports` map can override this value for a single entrypoint. |
| `exports` | json | Declarative exports for the Worker, keyed by export name. Worker entrypoint entries \(`type: worker`\) carry cache configuration for that entrypoint. Durable Object entries \(`type: durable-object`\) are also configured here and drive namespace reconciliation. At most 100 entries; class names are at most 128 characters. |
| `exports_reconciliation` | json | Summary of what the declarative exports reconciliation did on this upload. |
| `result_info` | json | Pagination metadata returned by Cloudflare |

### Cloudflare Update R2 Bucket Cors

Replaces the Cross-Origin Resource Sharing (CORS) rules for an R2 bucket. Rules specify which origins, methods, and headers are allowed for browser requests to objects in the bucket. Replaces the configuration; resend all values to retain.

#### Input

| Parameter | Type | Required | Description |
| --------- | ---- | -------- | ----------- |
| `accountId` | string | Yes | Account id |
| `bucketName` | string | Yes | Bucket name |
| `apiKey` | string | Yes | Cloudflare API token |
| `rules` | json | No | JSON array. rules Fields: allowed, exposeHeaders, id, maxAgeSeconds. |
| `jurisdiction` | string | No | Jurisdiction where objects in this bucket are guaranteed to be stored. |

#### Output

| Parameter | Type | Description |
| --------- | ---- | ----------- |
| `success` | boolean | Whether Cloudflare accepted the operation |

### Cloudflare Update R2 Bucket Lifecycle

Replaces the object lifecycle rules for an R2 bucket. Rules match object-key prefixes and can expire objects, abort incomplete multipart uploads, or transition objects to Infrequent Access storage. Replaces the configuration; resend all values to retain.

#### Input

| Parameter | Type | Required | Description |
| --------- | ---- | -------- | ----------- |
| `accountId` | string | Yes | Account id |
| `bucketName` | string | Yes | Bucket name |
| `apiKey` | string | Yes | Cloudflare API token |
| `rules` | json | No | JSON array. rules Fields: abortMultipartUploadsTransition, conditions, deleteObjectsTransition, enabled, id, storageClassTransitions. |
| `jurisdiction` | string | No | Jurisdiction where objects in this bucket are guaranteed to be stored. |

#### Output

| Parameter | Type | Description |
| --------- | ---- | ----------- |
| `success` | boolean | Whether Cloudflare accepted the operation |

### Cloudflare Update Rate Limiting Rule

Updates a rate limiting rule in the http_ratelimit phase entry point ruleset of a zone, using the current Rulesets-based rate limiting API. Cloudflare replaces the rule definition rather than merging it, so send the complete rule — every field you omit is reset. Run "List Rate Limiting Rules" first to read the current definition and get the ruleset ID. Requires an API token with Zone WAF Edit.

#### Input

| Parameter | Type | Required | Description |
| --------- | ---- | -------- | ----------- |
| `exposed_credential_check` | json | No | JSON object. Configuration for exposed credential checking. Fields: password_expression, username_expression. |
| `position` | json | No | JSON object. An object configuring where the rule will be placed. Fields: before, after, index. |
| `score_per_period` | number | No | The score threshold per period for which the action will be executed the first time. |
| `score_response_header_name` | string | No | A response header name provided by the origin, which contains the score to increment rate limit counter with. |
| `dry_run` | boolean | No | dry run |
| `zoneId` | string | Yes | The zone ID that owns the rule |
| `rulesetId` | string | Yes | The http_ratelimit entry point ruleset ID, as returned by "List Rate Limiting Rules" |
| `ruleId` | string | Yes | The rate limiting rule ID to update |
| `expression` | string | Yes | Cloudflare filter expression selecting the requests the rule applies to |
| `characteristics` | string | Yes | Comma-separated counting characteristics. cf.colo.id is mandatory. ip.src and cf.unique_visitor_id are mutually exclusive — include at most one. |
| `period` | number | Yes | Counting window in seconds. Cloudflare accepts only 10, 60, 120, 300, 600, or 3600 |
| `requestsPerPeriod` | number | Yes | Number of requests allowed within the counting period before the action fires |
| `action` | string | Yes | Action applied once the limit is exceeded: block, managed_challenge, js_challenge, challenge, or log. Required because this endpoint replaces the rule rather than merging into it — a defaulted action would silently convert an existing log or challenge rule into a hard block |
| `mitigationTimeout` | number | No | Seconds the action stays applied. Cloudflare accepts only 0, 10, 60, 120, 300, 600, 3600, or 86400 |
| `counting_expression` | string | No | Optional expression defining which requests are counted |
| `requestsToOrigin` | boolean | No | When true, only requests that reach the origin are counted |
| `description` | string | No | Human-readable description of the rule |
| `enabled` | boolean | No | Whether the rule is enabled |
| `ref` | string | No | Reference tag that stays stable across rule updates. Because the update replaces the rule, omitting it resets the tag to the rule ID and breaks anything matching on the old value |
| `actionParameters` | string | No | JSON object of action-specific parameters for the mitigation action, e.g. \{"response":\{"status_code":429,"content":"\{\"error\":\"rate limited\"\}","content_type":"application/json"\}\} for a custom block response. Because the update replaces the rule, omitting it resets action_parameters to \{\} and the rule falls back to Cloudflare's default block page |
| `logging` | string | No | JSON logging configuration to preserve, e.g. \{"enabled":true\}. Omitting it on a rule that had logging configured resets it to the default |
| `apiKey` | string | Yes | Cloudflare API Token |

| `max` | json | Per-metric DNS analytics maximums. Cloudflare documents this as currently always an empty object |

### Cloudflare Update Ruleset

Updates a zone ruleset, creating a new version. Replaces the configuration; resend all values to retain.

#### Input

| Parameter | Type | Required | Description |
| --------- | ---- | -------- | ----------- |
| `zoneId` | string | Yes | Zone id |
| `rulesetId` | string | Yes | Ruleset id |

| `kind` | string | No | The kind of the ruleset. Allowed values: managed, custom, root, zone. |
| `phase` | string | No | The phase of the ruleset. Allowed values: ddos_l4, ddos_l7, http_config_settings, http_custom_errors, http_log_custom_fields, http_ratelimit, http_request_cache_settings, http_request_dynamic_redirect, http_request_firewall_custom, http_request_firewall_managed, http_request_late_transform, http_request_origin, http_request_redirect, http_request_sanitize, http_request_sbfm, http_request_transform, http_response_cache_settings, http_response_compression, http_response_firewall_custom, http_response_firewall_managed, http_response_headers_transform, magic_transit, magic_transit_ids_managed, magic_transit_managed, magic_transit_ratelimit. |
| `rules` | json | No | JSON array. The list of rules in the ruleset. Fields: action, action_parameters, categories, description, enabled, exposed_credential_check, expression, id, last_updated, logging, ratelimit, ref, version. |
| `dry_run` | boolean | No | Validates the request without persisting changes when set to `true`. Responses that normally return 200 return `result: null`; endpoints that normally return 204 continue to return 204. |

### Cloudflare Update Ruleset Rule

Updates a rule in a zone ruleset. Cloudflare replaces the rule definition rather than merging it, so you must send every field you want the rule to keep — any field you omit is reset to its default. Read the current rule with "Get Ruleset" first. Requires an API token with Zone WAF Edit (or another matching ruleset Write permission).

#### Input

| Parameter | Type | Required | Description |
| --------- | ---- | -------- | ----------- |
| `exposed_credential_check` | json | No | JSON object. Configuration for exposed credential checking. Fields: password_expression, username_expression. |
| `position` | json | No | JSON object. An object configuring where the rule will be placed. Fields: before, after, index. |
| `dry_run` | boolean | No | dry run |
| `zoneId` | string | Yes | The zone ID that owns the ruleset |
| `rulesetId` | string | Yes | The ruleset ID containing the rule |
| `ruleId` | string | Yes | The rule ID to update |
| `action` | string | Yes | The action the rule performs, e.g. block, challenge, js_challenge, managed_challenge, log, skip, or execute. Required because this endpoint replaces the rule definition — omitting it resets the stored action |
| `expression` | string | Yes | Cloudflare filter expression selecting matching requests. Required because this endpoint replaces the rule definition — omitting it resets the stored expression |
| `description` | string | No | Human-readable description of the rule |
| `enabled` | boolean | No | Whether the rule is enabled |
| `ref` | string | No | Reference tag that stays stable across rule updates. Because the update replaces the rule, omitting it resets the tag to the rule ID and breaks anything matching on the old value |
| `actionParameters` | string | No | JSON object of action-specific parameters, e.g. \{"id":"&lt;MANAGED_RULESET_ID&gt;","overrides":\{"rules":\[\{"id":"&lt;RULE_ID&gt;","action":"log","enabled":true,"score_threshold":40\}\]\}\}. Required on an execute rule and must be sent on every update: the endpoint replaces the rule, so omitting it resets action_parameters to \{\} — which unbinds the managed ruleset the rule deploys and every override under it |
| `ratelimit` | string | No | JSON rate limiting configuration to preserve on a rule in the http_ratelimit phase, e.g. \{"characteristics":\["cf.colo.id","ip.src"\],"period":60,"requests_per_period":100\}. Because the update replaces the rule, omitting this on a rate limiting rule stops it rate limiting |
| `logging` | string | No | JSON logging configuration to preserve, e.g. \{"enabled":true\}. Omitting it on a rule that had logging configured resets it to the default |
| `apiKey` | string | Yes | Cloudflare API Token |

#### Output

| Parameter | Type | Description |
| --------- | ---- | ----------- |
| `zones` | json | List of zones/domains |
| `records` | json | List of DNS records |
| `certificates` | json | List of SSL/TLS certificate packs |
| `settings` | json | DNS record settings, Worker configuration, or zone settings depending on the operation |
| `unreadable` | json | Requested zone settings Cloudflare refused, with the reason for each |
| `totals` | json | Aggregate DNS analytics totals |
| `min` | json | Per-metric DNS analytics minimums. Cloudflare documents this as currently always an empty object |
| `max` | json | Per-metric DNS analytics maximums. Cloudflare documents this as currently always an empty object |
| `query` | json | Echo of the DNS analytics query parameters sent |
| `data` | json | Structured DNS record data, or DNS analytics rows depending on the operation |
| `data_lag` | number | Processing lag in seconds before analytics data becomes available |
| `rows` | number | Total number of rows in the DNS analytics result set |
| `id` | string | Resource ID |
| `name` | string | Resource name |
| `status` | string | Resource status |
| `paused` | boolean | Whether the zone is paused |
| `type` | string | Zone or record type |
| `name_servers` | json | Assigned Cloudflare name servers |
| `original_name_servers` | json | Original registrar name servers |

### Cloudflare Update Tunnel

Updates the name or secret of an existing Cloudflare Tunnel.

#### Input

| `accountId` | string | Yes | Account id |

| `tunnel_secret` | string | No | Sets the password required to run a locally-managed tunnel. Must be at least 32 bytes and encoded as a base64 string. |

### Cloudflare Update Tunnel Configuration

Replaces the configuration for a remotely managed Cloudflare Tunnel, including its ingress rules and origin request settings. Replaces the configuration; resend all values to retain.

| `cors_headers` | json | cors headers |

| `eager_redirect_cookie_setting` | boolean | Preemptively sets the Access session cookie on every hostname in a multi-hostname self-hosted application during the initial redirect chain, rather than setting it lazily on first visit. Defaults to true. Set to false to disable the eager redirect cookie behavior. |
| `enable_binding_cookie` | boolean | Enables the binding cookie, which increases security against compromised authorization tokens and CSRF attacks. |
| `http_only_cookie_attribute` | boolean | Enables the HttpOnly cookie attribute, which increases security against XSS attacks. |
| `mfa_config` | json | Configures multi-factor authentication \(MFA\) settings. |
| `options_preflight_bypass` | boolean | Allows options preflight requests to bypass Access authentication and go directly to the origin. Cannot turn on if cors_headers is set. |
| `path_cookie_attribute` | boolean | Enables cookie paths to scope an application's JWT to the application path. If disabled, the JWT will scope to the hostname by default |
| `read_service_tokens_from_header` | string | Allows matching Access Service Tokens passed HTTP in a single header with this name. |

| `service_auth_401_redirect` | boolean | Returns a 401 status code when the request is blocked by a Service Auth policy. |
| `skip_interstitial` | boolean | Enables automatic authentication through cloudflared. |

This allows users to access private domains without connecting to Gateway. The option requires Clientless Browser Isolation to be set up with policies that allow users of this application. |
